# Faction Changelog: data version 963 (2026-09-30)

Source: https://gdmissions.app/11th/rules/faction-changelog

Faction changes across 28 factions.

## Adepta Sororitas

### Castigator

- Castigator: increased additional selection cost (+10→+20)
  - Note: from the 3rd selection onwards

### Celestian Insidiants

- Celestian Insidiants: decreased points (120→115)

### Celestian Sacresants

- Celestian Sacresants: gained "Celestian" keyword

### Exorcist

- Exorcist: updated additionalCost: Additional selection cost of +40 pts now applies after 2 selections (previously after 1 selection)

### Morvenn Vahl

- Morvenn Vahl: increased points (200→215)

### Paragon Warsuits

- Paragon Warsuits: decreased points (180→165)

### Saint Celestine

- Saint Celestine: decreased points (150→135)

### "Hallowed Martyrs" (detachment)

- "Hallowed Martyrs" (detachment): updated force disposition (Priority Assets→Take and Hold)

## Adeptus Astartes

### Adrax Agatone

- Adrax Agatone: increased points (80→90)
- Adrax Agatone: increased Toughness (4→5)
- Adrax Agatone: updated "Drakkis" ranged weapon profile (S: 4→5, Keywords: added [CLOSE-QUARTERS], Keywords: removed [Pistol, Ignores Cover])
- Adrax Agatone: updated "Drakkis" ranged weapon profile (S: 4→5, Keywords: added [CLOSE-QUARTERS], Keywords: removed [Pistol, Ignores Cover])
- Adrax Agatone: updated "Drakkis" ranged weapon profile (S: 4→5, Keywords: added [CLOSE-QUARTERS], Keywords: removed [Pistol, Ignores Cover])
- Adrax Agatone: gained [DEVASTATING WOUNDS] keyword on "Malleus Noctum" melee weapon
- Adrax Agatone: gained "Transhuman Strategist" faction ability
- Adrax Agatone: gained "Combat Doctrines" faction ability
- Adrax Agatone: lost "Oath of Moment" faction ability
- Adrax Agatone: updated "Lord of the Pyroclasts" ability
  - Before: While an enemy unit is within Engagement Range of this model, halve the Objective Control characteristic of models in that enemy unit.
  - After: While an enemy unit is engaged with this unit, that enemy unit has -1 OC .
- Adrax Agatone: updated "Unto the Anvil" ability
  - Before: While this model is leading a unit, each time a model in that unit makes a melee attack, you can re-roll the Wound roll.
  - After: This unit’s melee attacks can: Re-roll wound rolls of 1. Or: If the assault doctrine is active for this unit, re-roll wound rolls .
- Adrax Agatone: gained "Explosives" keyword
- Adrax Agatone: lost "Adrax Agatone" keyword
- Adrax Agatone: gained attachment to Vanguard Veteran Squad (Leader)
- Adrax Agatone: lost attachment to Tactical Squad (Leader)
- Adrax Agatone: updated unit composition
  - Before: 1 Adrax Agatone – EPIC HERO
  - After: 1 Adrax Agatone model This model is equipped with: 1 Drakkis; 1 Malleus Noctum.
- Adrax Agatone: updated loadout
  - Before: This model is equipped with: Drakkis; Malleus Noctum.

### Aethon Shaan

- Aethon Shaan: increased points (100→105)
- Aethon Shaan: increased Toughness (4→5)
- Aethon Shaan: updated "Heavy Bolt Pistol" ranged weapon profile (S: 4→5, Keywords: added [CLOSE-QUARTERS], Keywords: removed [Pistol])
- Aethon Shaan: updated "Heavy Bolt Pistol" ranged weapon profile (S: 4→5, Keywords: added [CLOSE-QUARTERS], Keywords: removed [Pistol])
- Aethon Shaan: updated "Heavy Bolt Pistol" ranged weapon profile (S: 4→5, Keywords: added [CLOSE-QUARTERS], Keywords: removed [Pistol])
- Aethon Shaan: updated "Claws of Severax" melee weapon profile (Keywords: added [SUSTAINED HITS 2: non-MONSTER/VEHICLE], Keywords: removed [Sustained Hits 2])
- Aethon Shaan: updated "Claws of Severax" melee weapon profile (Keywords: added [SUSTAINED HITS 2: non-MONSTER/VEHICLE], Keywords: removed [Sustained Hits 2])
- Aethon Shaan: gained "Transhuman Strategist" faction ability
- Aethon Shaan: gained "Combat Doctrines" faction ability
- Aethon Shaan: lost "Oath of Moment" faction ability
- Aethon Shaan: updated "Master of Shadows" ability
  - Before: In your Command phase, you can select one unit from your opponent’s army. Until the start of your next Command phase, each time an ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit from your army declares a charge while it is within 12" of that enemy unit, you can re-roll the Charge roll, but it must declare that enemy unit as a target of that charge (if possible).
  - After: In your Command phase, you can select one friendly Adeptus Astartes Infantry unit. You can re-roll charge rolls for that unit until the start of your next Command phase.
- Aethon Shaan: gained "Blackwing Mantle (Once per phase, per army)" ability
  - Before: You can target this unit with the Rapid Ingress / Heroic Intervention stratagem, regardless of any other … phase. If you do: ■ That use is -1 CP. ■ That use does not …
  - After: You can target this unit with the Heroic Intervention stratagem , regardless of any other … phase. If you do: That use is -1 CP. That use does not …
- Aethon Shaan: lost "Blackwing Mantle" ability
  - Before: You can target this unit with the Rapid Ingress / Heroic Intervention stratagem, regardless of any other … phase. If you do: ■ That use is -1 CP. ■ That use does not …
  - After: You can target this unit with the Heroic Intervention stratagem , regardless of any other … phase. If you do: That use is -1 CP. That use does not …
- Aethon Shaan: lost "Chapter Master of the Raven Guard" ability
  - Before: At the start of the Declare Battle Formations step, if your army includes AETHON SHAAN and KAYVAAN SHRIKE, until the end of the battle, your KAYVAAN SHRIKE unit loses its Lone Operative ability and it replaces its CHAPTER MASTER keyword with CAPTAIN.
- Aethon Shaan: gained "Explosives" keyword
- Aethon Shaan: gained "Smoke" keyword
- Aethon Shaan: lost "Aethon Shaan" keyword
- Aethon Shaan: updated unit composition
  - Before: 1 Aethon Shaan – EPIC HERO
  - After: 1 Aethon Shaan model This model is equipped with: 1 Claws of Severax; 1 Heavy Bolt Pistol.
- Aethon Shaan: updated loadout
  - Before: This model is equipped with: 1 heavy bolt pistol; 1 Claws of Severax

### Drop Pod

- Drop Pod: updated Objective Control (0→-)

- Drop Pod: updated "Deployment Complete" ability
  - Before: Once this unit is set up on the battlefield and all units within it have disembarked, until the end of the battle, units cannot embark within this TRANSPORT.
  - After: When this unit is set up and all units embarked within it have disembarked, units cannot embark within this unit.
- Drop Pod: updated "Drop Pod Assault" ability
  - Before: This model must start the battle in Reserves and can be set up in the Reinforcements step of your first, second or third Movement phase, regardless of any mission rules. Any units embarked within this model must immediately disembark after it has been set up on the battlefield, and they must be set up … away from all enemy models.
  - After: This unit must start the battle in strategic reserves . In your first Movement phase, this unit can make an ingress move . When this unit is set up, all units embarked within this unit must make a disembark/assault disembark move (pg 157), and those units must be set up … away from all enemy units.
- Drop Pod: lost "Combat Disembarkation" ability
  - Before: Each time a unit disembarks from this model after it has been set up on the battlefield, that unit is still eligible to declare a charge this turn.
- Drop Pod: gained "TRANSPORT" ability
  - After: This model has a transport capacity of 12 Adeptus Astartes Infantry models. It cannot transport Gravis/Jump Pack/Terminator models.
- Drop Pod: lost "Designer’s Note" ability
  - Before: *The highlighted portions of this model are the only parts that are considered to make up its hull. Models can be set up or end a move on any part of this model that is not highlighted in red. If any models are on non-highlighted sections of this model when it is destroyed, place those models as close to their original position as possible, on the battlefield, after removing this model.*
- Drop Pod: lost "Drop Pod" keyword
- Drop Pod: updated unit composition

- Drop Pod: updated base size
- Drop Pod: updated transport

When resolving these attacks, any Hazardous tests taken for that attack are automatically passed. *Designer’s Note: This ability is triggered even when a model in this unit is destroyed as the result of failing a Hazardous test, meaning such a model may be able to shoot twice in the same phase.*

At the start of your Command phase, after you have selected your Oath of Moment target, select a second enemy unit. Once per battle round, one unit from your army with this ability can use it when a friendly ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit within 12" of that model is targeted with a Stratagem. If it does, reduce the CP cost of that usage of that Stratagem by 1CP.

That combat doctrine is active for that unit in addition to any other combat doctrines that are active for that unit until the start of your next Command phase.

### "Combat Doctrines" (army rule)

- "Combat Doctrines" (army rule): added army rule
  - Before: … you can select one of the Combat Doctrines listed below. Until the start of your next Command phase, that Combat Doctrine is active and its effects apply to all ADEPTUS ASTARTES units from your army. You can only select each Combat Doctrine once per battle. DEVASTATOR DOCTRINE This unit is eligible to shoot in a turn in which it Advanced. TACTICAL DOCTRINE This unit is eligible to shoot and declare a charge in a turn in which it Fell Back. ASSAULT DOCTRINE This unit is eligible to declare a charge in a turn in which it Advanced.
  - After: … you can select one combat doctrine listed below. If you do, that combat doctrine is active for friendly Adeptus Astartes units with this ability until the start of your next Command phase. Assault Doctrine When this unit is selected to make an advance move , that advance move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to declare a charge . Devastator Doctrine This unit’s ranged attacks have [Assault] . Tactical Doctrine When this unit is selected to make a fall-back move , that fall-back move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to shoot and eligible to declare a charge . Unless otherwise stated: You can only select each combat doctrine once per battle. Only one combat doctrine can be active for each unit. If a rule makes a combat doctrine active for a unit, any combat doctrine previously active for that unit is no longer active for that unit.

### "Oath of Moment" (army rule)

- "Oath of Moment" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: If your Army Faction is ADEPTUS ASTARTES, at the start of your Command phase, select one unit from your opponent’s army. Until the start of your next Command phase, that enemy unit is your Oath of Moment target. Each time a model with this ability makes an attack that targets your Oath of Moment target: ■ You can re-roll the Hit roll. ■ If you are using a Codex: Space Marines Detachment and your army does not include one or more units with the BLACK TEMPLARS, BLOOD ANGELS, DARK ANGELS, DEATHWATCH or SPACE WOLVES keywords, or one or more units from those factions' Munitorum Field Manual sections, add 1 to the Wound roll as well.

### "Space Marine Chapters" (army rule)

- "Space Marine Chapters" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: ■ If an ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit has a second Faction keyword on its datasheet, that Faction keyword is the name of that unit’s Chapter. For example, Marneus Calgar has both the ADEPTUS ASTARTES and ULTRAMARINES Faction keywords, and is therefore from the Ultramarines Chapter. ■ You cannot include units from more than one Chapter in your army. ■ If your army includes one or more BLACK TEMPLARS units, it cannot include any ADEPTUS ASTARTES PSYKER models, and cannot include any of the following models that do not have the Black Templars keyword: GLADIATOR LANCER; GLADIATOR REAPER; GLADIATOR VALIANT; IMPULSOR; REPULSOR; REPULSOR EXECUTIONER. ■ If your army includes one or more SPACE WOLVES units, it cannot include any of the following units: APOTHECARY; DEVASTATOR SQUAD; TACTICAL SQUAD. DEATHWATCH ■ Your army can include ADEPTUS ASTARTES DEATHWATCH units, but it cannot include any ADEPTUS ASTARTES units drawn from any other Chapter. ■ With the exception of KILL TEAM CASSIUS (see *Legends: Agents of the Imperium*), your army cannot include any AGENTS OF THE IMPERIUM DEATHWATCH units. ■ Your army cannot include any of the following units: ASSAULT SQUAD; ASSAULT SQUAD WITH JUMP PACKS; ATTACK BIKE SQUAD; DEVASTATOR SQUAD; LAND SPEEDER STORM; RELIC TERMINATOR SQUAD; SCOUT BIKE SQUAD; SCOUT SQUAD; SCOUT SNIPER SQUAD; TACTICAL SQUAD; TERMINATOR ASSAULT SQUAD; TERMINATOR SQUAD.

### "A Deadly Prize" (stratagem)

- "A Deadly Prize" (stratagem): removed stratagem
  - Before: Start of the Command phase. One ADEPTUS ASTARTES INFANTRY or ADEPTUS ASTARTES MOUNTED unit from your army within range of an objective marker you control. That objective marker is said to be Sabotaged, and remains under your control even if you have no models within range of it, until your opponent controls it at the start or end of any turn. While an objective marker is Sabotaged and under your control, each time an enemy unit ends a Normal, Advance, Fall Back or Charge move within range of that objective marker, roll one D6: on a 2+, that enemy unit suffers D3 mortal wounds.

### "Adaptive Operations" (stratagem)

- "Adaptive Operations" (stratagem): removed stratagem
  - Before: Your Shooting phase, when a friendly Phobos/Scout Squad unit starts an action . That Phobos / Scout Squad unit. That action does not prevent your unit from being eligible to shoot .

### "Advanced Deployment" (stratagem)

- "Advanced Deployment" (stratagem): removed stratagem
  - Before: Your Movement phase. One ADEPTUS ASTARTES TRANSPORT unit from your army that has not been selected to move this phase. Until the end of the phase, units can disembark from your TRANSPORT after it has Advanced. Units that do so count as having made a Normal move this phase, and cannot declare a charge in the same turn (unless your TRANSPORT has the Assault Ramp ability), but can otherwise act normally.

### "Angels Defiant" (stratagem)

- "Angels Defiant" (stratagem): removed stratagem
  - Before: Your opponent’s Shooting phase or the Fight phase, just after an enemy unit has selected its targets. One ADEPTUS ASTARTES BATTLELINE unit from your army that was selected as the target of one or more of the attacking unit’s attacks. Until the end of the phase, each time an attack targets your unit, if the Strength characteristic of that attack is greater than the Toughness characteristic of your unit, subtract 1 from the Wound roll.

### "Anti-grav Surge" (stratagem)

- "Anti-grav Surge" (stratagem): removed stratagem
  - Before: End of your opponent’s Fight phase. One friendly unengaged Speeder unit. Place your unit in strategic reserves .

### "Architect of War" (enhancement)

- "Architect of War" (enhancement): removed enhancement
  - Before: ADEPTUS ASTARTES model only. While the bearer is leading a unit, ranged weapons equipped by models in that unit have the [IGNORES COVER] ability.

### "Armoured Commander" (enhancement)

- "Armoured Commander" (enhancement): removed enhancement
  - Before: ADEPTUS ASTARTES model only. Once per turn, in your Movement phase, the bearer can use this Enhancement. If it does, select one friendly ADEPTUS ASTARTES TRANSPORT that is in Strategic Reserves. Until the end of the phase, for the purposes of setting up that TRANSPORT on the battlefield, treat the current battle round number as being one higher than it actually is.

### "Astartes Tank Ace (Aura)" (enhancement)

- "Astartes Tank Ace (Aura)" (enhancement): removed enhancement
  - Before: ADEPTUS ASTARTES VEHICLE model only. In your Shooting phase, while a friendly ADEPTUS ASTARTES VEHICLE unit is within 6" of the bearer, ranged weapons equipped by models in that unit have the [ASSAULT] ability

### "Augmetic Fortitude" (stratagem)

- "Augmetic Fortitude" (stratagem): removed stratagem
  - Before: Your opponent’s Charge phase, just after an enemy unit ends a Charge move. One ADEPTUS ASTARTES TERMINATOR, BLADEGUARD VETERAN SQUAD, STERNGUARD VETERAN SQUAD or VANGUARD VETERAN SQUAD unit from your army within Engagement Range of that enemy unit. - removed: [Codex: Space Marines] While using the Gladius Task Force Detachment, does a Combat Doctrine need to be active for my army…
  - Before: No.
- removed: [Codex: Space Marines] If a Hellblaster is destroyed by anything other than an attack or Hazardous test, can that model us…
  - Before: No.
- removed: [Codex: Space Marines] Can I use a Captain’s Rites of Battle ability to target that Captain’s unit if it is in Reserves or…
  - Before: Yes.
- removed: [Codex: Space Marines] Does an objective marker that has been Sabotaged by my army using the A Deadly Prize Stratagem rema…
  - Before: Yes. However, while your opponent controls that objective marker, its ability to inflict mortal wounds is not active.
- removed: [Codex: Space Marines] While an Attached unit is embarked within a TRANSPORT, do weapons that TRANSPORT shoots wit…
  - Before: No. Firing Deck enables a TRANSPORT to be equipped with a number of weapons equipped by its passengers (Core Rules), but the TRANSPORT itself is not part of that Attached unit.
- removed: [Codex: Space Marines] Can a unit embarked within a TRANSPORT be selected as the Oath of Moment target?
  - Before: Yes.
- removed: [Codex: Space Marines] Can a unit in Reserves be selected as the Oath of Moment target?
  - Before: Yes.
- removed: [Codex: Space Marines] Can a unit in Reserves use an Enhancement (following all restrictions outlined by that Enhancement)?
  - Before: Yes.
- removed: [Codex: Space Marines] If I have two or more Impulsors within 6" of a unit I target with a Stratagem, do each of their Orb…
  - Before: No, they are Aura abilities and so do not stack.
- removed: [Codex: Space Marines] If I use a Stratagem on a unit within 6" of an Impulsor with an Orbital Comms Array, but I do not r…
  - Before: No.
- removed: [Codex: Space Marines] Do multiple instances of the Astartes Banner ability stack?
  - Before: Yes.
- removed: [Codex: Space Marines] If I select one MONSTER or VEHICLE unit as the target of all of a Stormspeeder Thunderstrik…
  - Before: No. Only after you have resolved the attacks with all weapons it is shooting with this phase, has that model shot (see ‘Shot’ in the Rules Appendix, 04.03.04).
- removed: [Codex: Space Marines] When making attacks with a unit from my army, I split my attacks between the first Oath of Moment t…
  - Before: No. Only after all of the attacks from that unit have been resolved will the second Oath of Moment selection become active.

### Terminator Squad

### "Combat Doctrines" (army rule)

- "Combat Doctrines" (army rule): added army rule
  - After: At the start of your Command phase, you can select one combat doctrine listed below. If you do, that combat doctrine is active for friendly Adeptus Astartes units with this ability until the start of your next Command phase. Assault Doctrine When this unit is selected to make an advance move , that advance move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to declare a charge . Devastator Doctrine This unit’s ranged attacks have [Assault] . Tactical Doctrine When this unit is selected to make a fall-back move , that fall-back move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to shoot and eligible to declare a charge . Unless otherwise stated: You can only select each combat doctrine once per battle. Only one combat doctrine can be active for each unit. If a rule makes a combat doctrine active for a unit, any combat doctrine previously active for that unit is no longer active for that unit.

### "Heirs of Sigismund" (army rule)

- "Heirs of Sigismund" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: ■ If an ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit has a second Faction keyword on its datasheet, that Faction keyword is the name of that unit’s Chapter. For example, High Marshal Helbrecht has both the ADEPTUS ASTARTES and BLACK TEMPLARS Faction keywords, and is therefore from the Black Templars Chapter. ■ You cannot include units from more than one Chapter in your army. ■ If your army includes one or more units from the Black Templars Chapter or you are using one of the Detachments found on the following pages: □ ADEPTUS ASTARTES units from your army lose the Oath of Moment army rule (if they have it), and it is replaced with the Templar Vows army rule. □ You cannot use the 1st Company Task Force Detachment (see *Codex: Space Marines).* □ Your army cannot include any ADEPTUS ASTARTES PSYKER models. □ Your army cannot include the following datasheets from *Codex: Space Marines:* Gladiator Lancer; Gladiator Reaper; Gladiator Valiant; Impulsor; Land Raider Crusader; Repulsor; Repulsor Executioner; Sternguard Veteran Squad; Terminator Squad.

### "Space Marine Chapters" (army rule)

- "Space Marine Chapters" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: ■ If an ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit has a second Faction keyword on its datasheet, that Faction keyword is the name of that unit’s Chapter. For example, Marneus Calgar has both the ADEPTUS ASTARTES and ULTRAMARINES Faction keywords, and is therefore from the Ultramarines Chapter. ■ You cannot include units from more than one Chapter in your army. ■ If your army includes one or more BLACK TEMPLARS units, it cannot include any ADEPTUS ASTARTES PSYKER models, and cannot include any of the following models that do not have the Black Templars keyword: GLADIATOR LANCER; GLADIATOR REAPER; GLADIATOR VALIANT; IMPULSOR; REPULSOR; REPULSOR EXECUTIONER. ■ If your army includes one or more SPACE WOLVES units, it cannot include any of the following units: APOTHECARY; DEVASTATOR SQUAD; TACTICAL SQUAD. DEATHWATCH ■ Your army can include ADEPTUS ASTARTES DEATHWATCH units, but it cannot include any ADEPTUS ASTARTES units drawn from any other Chapter. ■ With the exception of KILL TEAM CASSIUS (see *Legends: Agents of the Imperium*), your army cannot include any AGENTS OF THE IMPERIUM DEATHWATCH units. ■ Your army cannot include any of the following units: ASSAULT SQUAD; ASSAULT SQUAD WITH JUMP PACKS; ATTACK BIKE SQUAD; DEVASTATOR SQUAD; LAND SPEEDER STORM; RELIC TERMINATOR SQUAD; SCOUT BIKE SQUAD; SCOUT SQUAD; SCOUT SNIPER SQUAD; TACTICAL SQUAD; TERMINATOR ASSAULT SQUAD; TERMINATOR SQUAD.

### "Templar Vows" (army rule)

- "Templar Vows" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: If your Army Faction is ADEPTUS ASTARTES, at the start of the first battle round, select one of the following Vows to be active for ADEPTUS ASTARTES units from your army. While a Vow is active for a unit from your army, that unit has the associated ability shown below. Abhor the Witch, Destroy … that target a PSYKER unit have [PRECISION]. When this unit declares a charge, If an enemy PSYKER unit is within 12" of this unit, you can use this part of this ability. If you do: This unit can re-roll that charge roll. This unit must end that charge move engaged with one or more of those enemy PSYKER units. Accept any Challenge, no Matter the Odds Each time a model in this unit makes a melee attack, if the Strength characteristic of that attack is less than or equal to the Toughness characteristic of the target, add 1 to the Wound roll. Suffer Not the Unclean to Live This unit is eligible to declare a charge in a turn in which it Fell Back, and each time a model in this unit makes a Pile-in or Consolidation move, it does not need to end that move closer to the closest enemy model, provided it ends that move as close as possible to the closest enemy unit. Uphold the Honour of the Emperor If this unit has the INFANTRY keyword: ■ At the end of your Command phase, if this unit is within range of an objective marker you control, that objective marker remains under your control until your opponent’s Level of Control over that objective marker is greater than yours at the end of a phase. ■ If the mission you are playing features Actions, this unit is eligible to start to perform an Action in a turn in which it Advanced.
  - After: At the start of the first battle round, you can select one Vow listed below. That Vow is active for friendly Adeptus Astartes units until the end of the battle. Abhor the Witch, Destroy … that target a PSYKER unit: Have [PRECISION] . Have [LANCE] . Accept Any Challenge, No Matter the Odds Friendly melee attacks that target a unit with a T greater than this unit’s S have +1 to wound rolls . Uphold the Honour of the Emperor Friendly Infantry units have: At the end of your Movement phase, if this unit is controlling an objective , that objective is secured . When this unit is selected to make an advance move , that advance move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to start an action . Restrictions: Your army can include Black Templars units, but it cannot include any Adeptus Astartes units drawn from any other Chapter.

- Astorath: updated "Redeemer of the Lost" ability
  - Before: While this model is leading a unit, each time a model in that unit is destroyed by a melee attack, if that model has not fought this phase, roll one D6. On a 4+, do not remove it from play; that destroyed model can fight after the attacking model’s unit has finished making its attacks, and is then removed from play.
  - After: In the Fight phase, when a model in this unit is destroyed , if this unit has not been selected to fight this phase, roll one D6, with +1 to that roll if that model was engaged with an enemy unit with a T greater than or equal to this unit's T : On a 4+, do not remove that model from the battlefield. When this unit has fought, or at the end of the phase (whichever comes first), that model is removed from the battlefield.

The assault doctrine is active for this unit in addition to any other combat doctrine until the start of your next Command phase. Start of your opponent's Movement phase, if this unit is not battle-shocked , you can make a psychic roll for this unit by rolling one D6. If you do: On a 1, this unit is battle-shocked . ### "Combat Doctrines" (army rule)

- "Combat Doctrines" (army rule): added army rule
  - After: At the start of your Command phase, you can select one combat doctrine listed below. If you do, that combat doctrine is active for friendly Adeptus Astartes units with this ability until the start of your next Command phase. Assault Doctrine When this unit is selected to make an advance move , that advance move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to declare a charge . Devastator Doctrine This unit’s ranged attacks have [Assault] . Tactical Doctrine When this unit is selected to make a fall-back move , that fall-back move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to shoot and eligible to declare a charge . Unless otherwise stated: You can only select each combat doctrine once per battle. Only one combat doctrine can be active for each unit. If a rule makes a combat doctrine active for a unit, any combat doctrine previously active for that unit is no longer active for that unit.

### "Oath of Moment" (army rule)

- "Oath of Moment" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: This ability is described in full on the Army Rules page of *Codex: Space Marines*.

### "Space Marine Chapters" (army rule)

- "Space Marine Chapters" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: ■ If an ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit has a second Faction keyword on its datasheet, that Faction keyword is the name of that unit’s Chapter. For example, Marneus Calgar has both the ADEPTUS ASTARTES and ULTRAMARINES Faction keywords, and is therefore from the Ultramarines Chapter. ■ You cannot include units from more than one Chapter in your army. ■ If your army includes one or more BLACK TEMPLARS units, it cannot include any ADEPTUS ASTARTES PSYKER models, and cannot include any of the following models that do not have the Black Templars keyword: GLADIATOR LANCER; GLADIATOR REAPER; GLADIATOR VALIANT; IMPULSOR; REPULSOR; REPULSOR EXECUTIONER. ■ If your army includes one or more SPACE WOLVES units, it cannot include any of the following units: APOTHECARY; DEVASTATOR SQUAD; TACTICAL SQUAD. DEATHWATCH ■ Your army can include ADEPTUS ASTARTES DEATHWATCH units, but it cannot include any ADEPTUS ASTARTES units drawn from any other Chapter. ■ With the exception of KILL TEAM CASSIUS (see *Legends: Agents of the Imperium*), your army cannot include any AGENTS OF THE IMPERIUM DEATHWATCH units. ■ Your army cannot include any of the following units: ASSAULT SQUAD; ASSAULT SQUAD WITH JUMP PACKS; ATTACK BIKE SQUAD; DEVASTATOR SQUAD; LAND SPEEDER STORM; RELIC TERMINATOR SQUAD; SCOUT BIKE SQUAD; SCOUT SQUAD; SCOUT SNIPER SQUAD; TACTICAL SQUAD; TERMINATOR ASSAULT SQUAD; TERMINATOR SQUAD.

### "The Sons of Sanguinius" (army rule)

- "The Sons of Sanguinius" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: ■ If an ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit has a second Faction keyword on its datasheet, that Faction keyword is the name of that unit’s Chapter. For example, Lemartes has both the ADEPTUS ASTARTES and BLOOD ANGELS Faction keywords, and is therefore from the Blood Angels Chapter. ■ You cannot include units from more than one Chapter in your army. *Designer’s Note: The rules presented in this section assume that the* ADEPTUS ASTARTES *units in your army are from the Blood Angels Chapter, but they can also be used to represent any Blood Angels successor Chapter, such as one described in the background section of this book, or even one of your own invention. However, players who wish to faithfully recreate the Blood Angels Chapter on the tabletop should only include* BLOOD ANGELS EPIC HEROES *if their collection is intended to represent the First Founding Chapter itself; Mephiston is the Chief Librarian of the Blood Angels, for example, and not of any of their successors.*

### "Transhuman Strategist" (army rule)

- "Transhuman Strategist" (army rule): added army rule
  - After: At the start of the battle round, if a model with this ability is your Warlord , gain 1 CP .

### "A Grim Warning" (stratagem)

- "A Grim Warning" (stratagem): removed stratagem
  - Before: Any phase. One BLOOD ANGELS unit from your army that was just destroyed while it was within range of one or more objective markers you controlled at the end of the previous phase. You can use this Stratagem on that unit even though it was just destroyed. Select one of those objective markers. That objective marker remains under your control until your opponent’s Level of Control over that objective marker is greater than yours at the end of a phase.

### "Aggressive Onslaught" (stratagem)

- "Aggressive Onslaught" (stratagem): removed stratagem
  - Before: Your Movement phase, just after an ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit from your army has Advanced. That ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit. Until the end of the turn, your unit is eligible to either shoot or declare a charge, even though it Advanced. You can instead choose for your unit to give in to the Red Thirst; if it does, it becomes Battle‐shocked (but the effects of this Stratagem still apply to it) and until the end of the turn, your unit is eligible to both shoot and declare a charge, even though it Advanced.

### "Combat Doctrines" (army rule)

- "Combat Doctrines" (army rule): added army rule
  - After: At the start of your Command phase, you can select one combat doctrine listed below. If you do, that combat doctrine is active for friendly Adeptus Astartes units with this ability until the start of your next Command phase. Assault Doctrine When this unit is selected to make an advance move , that advance move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to declare a charge . Devastator Doctrine This unit’s ranged attacks have [Assault] . Tactical Doctrine When this unit is selected to make a fall-back move , that fall-back move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to shoot and eligible to declare a charge . Unless otherwise stated: You can only select each combat doctrine once per battle. Only one combat doctrine can be active for each unit. If a rule makes a combat doctrine active for a unit, any combat doctrine previously active for that unit is no longer active for that unit.

### "Oath of Moment" (army rule)

- "Oath of Moment" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: This ability is described in full in the Army Rules section of *Codex: Space Marines*.

### "Space Marine Chapters" (army rule)

- "Space Marine Chapters" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: ■ If an ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit has a second Faction keyword on its datasheet, that Faction keyword is the name of that unit’s Chapter. For example, Marneus Calgar has both the ADEPTUS ASTARTES and ULTRAMARINES Faction keywords, and is therefore from the Ultramarines Chapter. ■ You cannot include units from more than one Chapter in your army. ■ If your army includes one or more BLACK TEMPLARS units, it cannot include any ADEPTUS ASTARTES PSYKER models, and cannot include any of the following models that do not have the Black Templars keyword: GLADIATOR LANCER; GLADIATOR REAPER; GLADIATOR VALIANT; IMPULSOR; REPULSOR; REPULSOR EXECUTIONER. ■ If your army includes one or more SPACE WOLVES units, it cannot include any of the following units: APOTHECARY; DEVASTATOR SQUAD; TACTICAL SQUAD. DEATHWATCH ■ Your army can include ADEPTUS ASTARTES DEATHWATCH units, but it cannot include any ADEPTUS ASTARTES units drawn from any other Chapter. ■ With the exception of KILL TEAM CASSIUS (see *Legends: Agents of the Imperium*), your army cannot include any AGENTS OF THE IMPERIUM DEATHWATCH units. ■ Your army cannot include any of the following units: ASSAULT SQUAD; ASSAULT SQUAD WITH JUMP PACKS; ATTACK BIKE SQUAD; DEVASTATOR SQUAD; LAND SPEEDER STORM; RELIC TERMINATOR SQUAD; SCOUT BIKE SQUAD; SCOUT SQUAD; SCOUT SNIPER SQUAD; TACTICAL SQUAD; TERMINATOR ASSAULT SQUAD; TERMINATOR SQUAD.

### "The Deathwing" (army rule)

- "The Deathwing" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: The following ADEPTUS ASTARTES units gain the DEATHWING keyword if they are drawn from the Dark Angels Chapter: ■ TERMINATOR units ■ BLADEGUARD ANCIENT, BLADEGUARD VETERAN SQUAD, STERNGUARD VETERAN SQUAD and VANGUARD VETERAN SQUAD WITH JUMP PACKS units ■ LAND RAIDER, LAND RAIDER CRUSADER, LAND RAIDER REDEEMER, REPULSOR and REPULSOR EXECUTIONER units ■ DREADNOUGHT units

### "The Ravenwing" (army rule)

- "The Ravenwing" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: The following ADEPTUS ASTARTES units gain the RAVENWING keyword if they are drawn from the Dark Angels Chapter: ■ MOUNTED units ■ VEHICLE units that can FLY

### "The Unforgiven" (army rule)

- "The Unforgiven" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: ■ If an ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit has a second Faction keyword on its datasheet, that Faction keyword is the name of that unit’s Chapter. For example, Asmodai has both the ADEPTUS ASTARTES and DARK ANGELS Faction keywords, and is therefore from the Dark Angels Chapter. ■ You cannot include units from more than one Chapter in your army. Designer’s Note: The rules presented in this section assume that the ADEPTUS ASTARTES units in your army are from the Dark Angels Chapter, but they can also be used to represent any Dark Angels successor Chapter, such as one described in the background section of this book, or even one of your own invention. However, players who wish to faithfully recreate the Dark Angels Chapter on the tabletop should only include DARK ANGELS EPIC HEROES if their collection is intended to represent the First Founding Chapter itself; Ezekiel is the Chief Librarian of the Dark Angels, for example, and not of any of their successors.

  - Before: ADEPTUS ASTARTES model only. Improve the Strength characteristic of melee weapons equipped by the bearer by 2, and improve the Armour Penetration and Damage characteristics of those weapons by 1.
  - After: Adeptus Astartes model only. This model’s melee attacks: Have +2 S . Have +1 AP .
- "Ancient Weapons" (enhancement): removed enhancement
  - Before: ADEPTUS ASTARTES model only. Improve the Strength characteristic of melee weapons equipped by the bearer by 2, and improve the Armour Penetration and Damage characteristics of those weapons by 1.
  - After: Adeptus Astartes model only. This model’s melee attacks: Have +2 S . Have +1 AP .

- "Armour of Contempt" (stratagem): removed stratagem
  - Before: Your opponent’s Shooting phase or the Fight phase, just after an enemy unit has selected its targets. One ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit from your army that was selected as the target of one or more of the attacking unit’s attacks. Until the attacking unit has finished making its attacks, each time an attack targets your unit, worsen the Armour Penetration characteristic of that attack by 1.

### "Combat Doctrines" (army rule)

- "Combat Doctrines" (army rule): added army rule
  - After: At the start of your Command phase, you can select one combat doctrine listed below. If you do, that combat doctrine is active for friendly Adeptus Astartes units with this ability until the start of your next Command phase. Assault Doctrine When this unit is selected to make an advance move , that advance move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to declare a charge . Devastator Doctrine This unit’s ranged attacks have [Assault] . Tactical Doctrine When this unit is selected to make a fall-back move , that fall-back move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to shoot and eligible to declare a charge . Unless otherwise stated: You can only select each combat doctrine once per battle. Only one combat doctrine can be active for each unit. If a rule makes a combat doctrine active for a unit, any combat doctrine previously active for that unit is no longer active for that unit.

### "Kill Teams" (army rule)

- "Kill Teams" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: Each time an attack targets a KILL TEAM unit from your army that contains models with different Toughness characteristics, until the attacking unit has finished making its attacks, use the Toughness characteristic of the majority of the models in that unit when determining what result is required for that attack to successfully wound. If two or more Toughness characteristics are tied for majority, use the highest Toughness characteristic. For the purposes of determining which models can embark within a TRANSPORT model, KILL TEAM models follow the normal restrictions listed on the TRANSPORT model.

### "Oath of Moment" (army rule)

- "Oath of Moment" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: If your Army Faction is ADEPTUS ASTARTES, at the start of your Command phase, select one unit from your opponent’s army. Until the start of your next Command phase, that enemy unit is your Oath of Moment target. Each time a model with this ability makes an attack that targets your Oath of Moment target: ■ You can re-roll the Hit roll. ■ If you are using a Codex: Space Marines Detachment and your army does not include one or more units with the BLACK TEMPLARS, BLOOD ANGELS, DARK ANGELS, DEATHWATCH or SPACE WOLVES keywords, or one or more units from those factions' Munitorum Field Manual sections, add 1 to the Wound roll as well.

### "Space Marine Chapters" (army rule)

- "Space Marine Chapters" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: ■ If an ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit has a second Faction keyword on its datasheet, that Faction keyword is the name of that unit’s Chapter. For example, Marneus Calgar has both the ADEPTUS ASTARTES and ULTRAMARINES Faction keywords, and is therefore from the Ultramarines Chapter. ■ You cannot include units from more than one Chapter in your army. ■ If your army includes one or more BLACK TEMPLARS units, it cannot include any ADEPTUS ASTARTES PSYKER models, and cannot include any of the following models that do not have the Black Templars keyword: GLADIATOR LANCER; GLADIATOR REAPER; GLADIATOR VALIANT; IMPULSOR; REPULSOR; REPULSOR EXECUTIONER. ■ If your army includes one or more SPACE WOLVES units, it cannot include any of the following units: APOTHECARY; DEVASTATOR SQUAD; TACTICAL SQUAD. DEATHWATCH ■ Your army can include ADEPTUS ASTARTES DEATHWATCH units, but it cannot include any ADEPTUS ASTARTES units drawn from any other Chapter. ■ With the exception of KILL TEAM CASSIUS (see *Legends: Agents of the Imperium*), your army cannot include any AGENTS OF THE IMPERIUM DEATHWATCH units. ■ Your army cannot include any of the following units: ASSAULT SQUAD; ASSAULT SQUAD WITH JUMP PACKS; ATTACK BIKE SQUAD; DEVASTATOR SQUAD; LAND SPEEDER STORM; RELIC TERMINATOR SQUAD; SCOUT BIKE SQUAD; SCOUT SQUAD; SCOUT SNIPER SQUAD; TACTICAL SQUAD; TERMINATOR ASSAULT SQUAD; TERMINATOR SQUAD.

### "Adaptive Tactics" (stratagem)

- "Adaptive Tactics" (stratagem): added stratagem

For each unit targeted, select Furor Tactics, Malleus Tactics or Purgatus Tactics. Until the start of your next Command phase, that Mission Tactic is active for that unit instead of any Mission Tactic that is active for your army.
  - After: Your Command phase. One friendly ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit. Select one combat doctrine . That combat doctrine is active for your unit until the start of your next Command phase.

### "Armour of Contempt" (stratagem)

- "Armour of Contempt" (stratagem): added stratagem
  - Before: Your opponent’s Shooting phase or the Fight phase, just after an enemy unit has selected its targets. One ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit from your army that was selected as the target of one or more of the attacking unit’s attacks. Until the attacking unit has finished making its attacks, each time an attack targets your unit, worsen the Armour Penetration characteristic of that attack by 1.
  - After: Your opponent’s Shooting phase or the Fight phase, when an enemy unit targets a friendly ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit. That ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit. Attacks that target your unit have -1 AP until that enemy unit has attacked.

  - After: Your Shooting phase, when a friendly LEGIONES DAEMONICA CHARACTER TZEENTCH unit (excluding MONSTER units) is selected to shoot . That LEGIONES DAEMONICA CHARACTER TZEENTCH unit. Your unit’s ranged attacks have [LETHAL HITS] .

### "skirling magicks" (stratagem)

- "skirling magicks" (stratagem): removed stratagem
  - Before: Your Shooting phase, when a friendly LEGIONES DAEMONICA CHARACTER TZEENTCH unit (excluding MONSTER units) is selected to shoot . That LEGIONES DAEMONICA CHARACTER TZEENTCH unit. Your unit’s ranged attacks have [LETHAL HITS] .

### "Thieves of Pain" (stratagem)

- "Thieves of Pain" (stratagem): removed stratagem
  - Before: Any phase, when an enemy unit targets a friendly LEGIONES DAEMONICA SLAANESH unit (excluding MONSTER/VEHICLE units), or when a friendly LEGIONES DAEMONICA SLAANESH unit (excluding MONSTER/VEHICLE units) suffers a mortal wound. That LEGIONES DAEMONICA SLAANESH unit. Select one other friendly LEGIONES DAEMONICA SLAANESH unit that is within 9" of and visible to your unit. Until the end of the phase, while the selected unit is on the battlefield, each time a model in your unit would lose a wound, inflict 1 mortal wound on the selected unit instead.

### "Combat Doctrines" (army rule)

- "Combat Doctrines" (army rule): added army rule
  - After: At the start of your Command phase, you can select one combat doctrine listed below. If you do, that combat doctrine is active for friendly Adeptus Astartes units with this ability until the start of your next Command phase. Assault Doctrine When this unit is selected to make an advance move , that advance move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to declare a charge . Devastator Doctrine This unit’s ranged attacks have [Assault] . Tactical Doctrine When this unit is selected to make a fall-back move , that fall-back move does not prevent this unit from being eligible to shoot and eligible to declare a charge . Unless otherwise stated: You can only select each combat doctrine once per battle. Only one combat doctrine can be active for each unit. If a rule makes a combat doctrine active for a unit, any combat doctrine previously active for that unit is no longer active for that unit.

### "Curse of the Wulfen" (army rule)

- "Curse of the Wulfen" (army rule): updated rules

  - After: While this unit is within 6” of a friendly Space Wolves Character model (excluding Wulfen models) or within 12” of a friendly Wolf Priest model, if this unit is not battle-shocked : If this unit has Infantry , this unit has +1 OC . If this unit has Vehicle , this unit has +3 OC . Bestial Forms: For the purposes of transport capacity , each Wulfen model takes up the space of 2 models.

### "Sons of Russ" (army rule)

- "Sons of Russ" (army rule): removed army rule
  - Before: ■ If an ADEPTUS ASTARTES unit has a second Faction keyword on its datasheet, that Faction keyword is the name of that unit’s Chapter. For example, Ragnar Blackmane has both the ADEPTUS ASTARTES and SPACE WOLVES Faction keywords, and is therefore from the Space Wolves Chapter. ■ You cannot include units from more than one Chapter in your army. ■ If your army uses any of the Detachments from this book, it can include SPACE WOLVES units, but it cannot include any ADEPTUS ASTARTES units drawn from any other Chapter. Designer’s Note: *The rules presented in this section assume that the* ADEPTUS ASTARTES *units in your army are from the Space Wolves Chapter, but they can also be used to represent any Space Wolves successor Chapter, such as one described in the background section of this book, or even one of your own invention. However, players who wish to faithfully recreate the Space Wolves Chapter on the tabletop should only include* SPACE WOLVES EPIC HEROES *if their collection is intended to represent the First Founding Chapter itself; Logan Grimnar is the Great Wolf of the Space Wolves, for example, and not of any of their successors.*

### "Countercharge" (stratagem)

- "Countercharge" (stratagem): removed stratagem
  - Before: End of your opponent’s Charge phase. One ADEPTUS ASTARTES CHARACTER unit from your army that is within 6" of one or more enemy units and would be eligible to declare a charge against one or more of those enemy units if it were your Charge phase. Your unit now declares a charge that targets only one or more of those enemy units, and you resolve that charge as if it were your Charge phase. Note that even if this charge is successful, your unit does not receive any Charge bonus this turn.
